# Marketing and Sales

[All categories](../README.md#categories) · 1,204 connectors · Last updated October 30, 2026

## Advertising and Paid Media

- [Ad Platform Updates](https://mcp-apps-muller.netlify.app/ad-updates/) **`C`** - Returns dated summaries of Meta and Google Ads changes from platform announcements, trade press and practitioner reports, each with a source link and verification level, refreshed daily. *Use case: Checking whether a newly announced Meta campaign feature has rolled out, finding what changed in Google Ads this week, looking for reports that explain a sudden cost jump.*
- [Ad Radar](https://ad-radar.dev) **`C`** - Searches a board of Meta (Facebook and Instagram) ads from DTC and e-commerce brands with at least $50k in estimated spend, reads full copy, transcripts and tags, shows which hooks, angles, formats and proof levels reach the top tier, profiles a brand's hook and angle mix, and generates a brief mapping a winning ad's structure onto a described product. *Use case: Finding top-spending skincare video ads that open with a problem hook, reviewing the hook mix a competitor brand is running, turning a winning ad into a creative brief for a new supplement.*
- [Ad Superpowers](https://adsuperpowers.ai) **`C`** - Reads and, with confirmation, writes across Meta, Google Ads, Analytics 4, Search Console, Tag Manager, Merchant Center, LinkedIn, and TikTok accounts, logging every action. *Use case: Answering one cross-channel performance question instead of opening eight dashboards, creating a new ad left paused for review, checking the audit log of recent changes.*
- [Ad Swipe File](https://adswipefile.com) **`C`** - Searches a curated library of real ads from brands on Meta, TikTok and other platforms by brand, niche and format, reads each ad's audiences and, on eligible plans, its copy, and lists saved and followed ads, with no spend or performance data. *Use case: Pulling recent UGC ads in the supplements niche, reading a skincare brand's testimonial ad copy, studying hooks before drafting new ad copy.*
- [AdAce Ads](https://adace.website) **`C`** - Reads live Google Ads and Meta performance, wasted spend, anomalies and cross-platform client overviews with GA4, Search Console and Klaviyo reports and a tracking audit, and proposes changes and creates paused campaigns, ad sets and ads that apply only after approval. *Use case: Finding search terms that spend without converting, proposing a budget shift between campaigns, creating a paused Meta campaign for review.*
- [AdBase](https://getadbase.com) **`C`** - Tracks competitors' Meta and Google ads, landing pages and email newsletters, searches and shows an ad library with creative tags and transcripts, and adds or removes watched advertisers. *Use case: Reviewing a competitor's longest-running Facebook ads, comparing landing page offers, summarizing what rivals promoted in newsletters this month.*
- [AdKit - Ads Manager](https://adkit.so) **`C`** - Manage campaigns, ad sets, keywords, audiences, and budgets across Meta, Google, TikTok, LinkedIn, Reddit, Microsoft, and X Ads, with reporting on spend, ROAS, and cost per result, PPC budget audits, a library of 500k+ competitor ads, and changes that stay drafts until approved. *Use case: Pulling last week's ROAS before a client report, pausing a campaign whose cost per result crossed a threshold, checking which competitor creatives have run longest before briefing a new set.*
- [AdLabs - Amazon Ads](https://adlabs.app) **`C`** - Reports Amazon Sponsored Products, Brands, Display, and DSP performance at several levels, audits account health, and applies bulk bid and targeting changes. *Use case: Finding search terms burning spend without sales, running an account health scorecard before a client call, applying a bulk bid cut to weak keywords.*
- [Adlane](https://adlane.app) **`C`** - Read-only review of connected Google and Meta ad accounts, campaign configuration and performance across date ranges. *Use case: Comparing last month's Google and Meta campaign results, checking a campaign's settings, spotting accounts with rising costs.*
- [Adlicio](https://tryadlicio.com) **`C`** - Turn customer comments into ad angles. *Use case: Pulling a recurring complaint from product reviews to find a new ad hook, turning a glowing customer quote into ad copy for a new campaign.*
- [AdLigner](https://adligner.com) **`C`** - Analyzes and manages Google, Microsoft, Meta, TikTok, LinkedIn, Reddit and ChatGPT ad accounts alongside GA4 and Klaviyo, finding wasted spend, pacing and creative fatigue, and creating, updating, pausing or resuming campaigns, ad groups, ads, audiences, experiments, automation rules, negative keywords and Klaviyo campaigns. *Use case: Finding wasted spend across Google search terms, comparing Meta conversions with GA4, pausing fatigued TikTok creatives.*
- [AdLupe](https://adlupe.com) **`C`** - Tracks competitors' ads from the public EU ad libraries of Meta and Google, labeling each as new, test, winner or steady, shows daily changes, compares competitors, searches ads and industry overviews, and adds or removes tracked competitors. *Use case: Checking which new ads a rival launched since yesterday, listing a competitor's ads running 30 days or more, comparing two competitors side by side.*
- [Admiral](https://meetadmiral.com) **`C`** - Reads Google Ads and Meta ad account data, including campaigns, costs, keywords, search terms, ads, alerts and trends, and files suggested changes such as pausing keywords or adding negatives into an approval queue without editing the ad platforms directly. *Use case: Finding keywords that spent money with no conversions last month, reviewing weekly cost trends, proposing a negative keyword for approval.*
- [Adology](https://adologyai.com) **`C`** - Competitive ad intelligence for marketers that pulls a brand's ad creative, hooks, and spend patterns across Meta, TikTok, YouTube, and more. *Use case: Checking a competitor's TikTok ad spend before planning next quarter's budget, pulling a rival's best-performing ad hooks on Meta for inspiration.*
- [Adomik Jean Pierre](https://www.adomik.com) **`C`** - Analyzes advertising and monetization data across sources, benchmarks performance against the market, and produces reports, forecasts, and summary decks. *Use case: Benchmarking yield against market rates, investigating an anomaly in yesterday's revenue, assembling a quarterly wrap-up deck for a publisher.*
- [adplane](https://adplane.ai) **`C`** - Google Ads and Meta Ads reporting and campaign management covering search terms, spend comparisons, GAQL queries, and creation of paused campaigns, ad groups, and creatives. *Use case: Comparing Google and Meta spend over the same date range, pulling search terms that wasted budget, building a paused search campaign for review before it runs.*
- [Ads MCP Audit](https://adsmcp.dev) **`C`** - Audits Google Ads accounts read-only for wasted spend, generates negative keyword and pause CSV files to import, compares results before and after a change, writes client reports, and runs read-only GAQL queries. *Use case: Finding search terms that spent budget without converting last month, exporting a negative keyword CSV for Google Ads Editor, checking whether a bid change lowered cost per conversion.*
- [Adsby MCP](https://adsby.co) **`C`** - Reports Google Ads campaign, ad group, keyword and search-term metrics, Meta Ads insights by account, campaign, ad set or ad with demographic, country and placement breakdowns, and Google Analytics traffic, acquisition, events and ecommerce purchases for accounts connected in an Adsby workspace. *Use case: Finding search terms with high cost and no conversions, breaking Meta purchase ROAS down by age and placement, checking which channels drove purchases last month.*
- [Adshelf](https://adshelf.app) **`C`** - Analyzes competitors' Facebook and Instagram ads archived from Meta's Ad Library, surfacing long-running creatives, hooks, angles, ad copy, and category benchmarks. *Use case: Breaking down a rival brand's creative strategy, pulling the hooks behind its longest-running ads, comparing a category's ad longevity before a launch.*
- [AdShields](https://www.adshields.pro/en) **`C`** - Creative operations hub: a library of videos and VSLs where your team writes scripts, comments by passage, versions, approves, and pushes to your ad platform — all in natural language. *Use case: Leaving a comment on a specific line of a VSL script before recording, approving a final video cut before it pushes live to the ad platform.*
- [Adside — Meta Ads, Google Ads & LinkedIn Ads reporting for agencies](https://adside.ai) **`C`** - Reads Meta, Google, and LinkedIn Ads performance data alongside a brand kit, tracked competitors, and saved team knowledge for reporting and on-brand copy. *Use case: Comparing cost per result across Meta and Google for last month, drafting on-brand ad headlines, reviewing what tracked competitors are running.*
- [Adsmom](https://adsmom.com) **`C`** - Searches competitor ads across Meta, TikTok, Google, YouTube, and LinkedIn, plus organic TikTok and Instagram content, with reach, estimated spend, and targeting data. *Use case: Reviewing a competitor's active creatives, estimating a rival brand's ad spend, finding which organic TikTok formats are gaining reach.*
- [Adspirer Ads & Performance Marketing Agent](https://www.adspirer.com) - Your performance marketing and paid media agent — an Agent that plans, launches, and autonomously runs your ad campaigns across Google, Meta, LinkedIn, TikTok, and more. *Use case: Launching a lead-gen campaign on LinkedIn before a product launch, shifting budget from an underperforming TikTok ad to a stronger one on Meta.*
- [Adszy](https://adszy.ai) **`C`** - Analyzes linked Google Ads accounts read-only, covering performance comparisons, search terms, negative-keyword candidates, wasted spend, budget pacing, anomalies, auction insights, products, feed issues and change history. *Use case: Finding the search terms that wasted spend last month, explaining why costs jumped last week.*
- [AdvisorPPC - Google Ads, Analytics, Tag Manager, Search Console and YouTube Connector](https://advisorppc.com) **`C`** - Google marketing tools in Claude. *Use case: Checking how a Google marketing campaign performed this week, pulling a quick report before a stakeholder meeting.*
- [AdWhispr Ads](https://adwhispr.com) - Searches brands' live Meta and TikTok ad libraries, builds competitive briefs, and launches, adjusts, and reports on Google, Meta, and TikTok campaigns. *Use case: Pulling a competitor's longest-running hooks, generating a positioning brief on a rival brand, launching a paused search campaign for review before it spends.*
- [Agency AI](https://www.agencyai.app) **`C`** - Meta and Google ad intelligence for Shopify stores, covering performance analysis, optimal architecture, budget strategy, and creative direction. *Use case: Checking why a Shopify store's Meta ad spend spiked this week, getting a budget recommendation before scaling up a Google campaign.*
- [Agency Autopilot](https://getagencyautopilot.com) **`C`** - Connects an agency's client Google Ads, Analytics 4, Search Console, Business Profile, Meta ads, WordPress and HighLevel CRM accounts to run reports, build branded client reports, manage campaigns, keywords, budgets and bidding, reply to reviews, post to Business Profiles, draft site pages and handle CRM leads, with changes previewed before confirmation. *Use case: Finding wasted spend and adding negative keywords for a client, building a monthly cross-channel report as a branded page, replying to new Business Profile reviews.*
- [Alison.AI](https://alison.ai) **`C`** - Analyzes ad creative performance across Meta, Google, TikTok, and other channels at the element level: hooks, colors, messaging, and pacing. *Use case: Finding which hook styles lifted click-through this month, spotting ads showing fatigue before spend is wasted, scoring a new creative ahead of launch.*
- [AMZ Vault](https://www.amz-vault.com) **`C`** - Reports Amazon settlement-based profit and margin per product and manages Sponsored Products, Brands, and Display campaigns, staging every change for explicit approval. *Use case: Checking margin after fees on last month's top-selling product, staging negative keywords for search terms that spent without orders, approving a staged bid change.*
- [Ativa Dash](https://ativadash.com) **`C`** - Reads Meta Ads and Google Ads spend, leads, CPL, CPA and ROAS by client, campaign, ad set and ad, compares periods, analyzes creatives and form leads and diagnoses broken UTMs, and with admin permission creates paused campaigns, edits ads, pauses weak campaigns, scales winners and adjusts budgets after confirmation. *Use case: Comparing a client's cost per lead with last month, finding fatigued creatives, pausing underperforming Meta campaigns.*
- [Audienzes](https://audienzes.com) **`C`** - Reads live ad account and campaign data, including status, impressions, clicks, spend and daily trends, across Meta, TikTok, LinkedIn, Pinterest, Snapchat, Google Ads, Reddit, Spotify, Amazon Ads and X Ads. *Use case: Comparing spend across TikTok and Snapchat campaigns this week, checking which LinkedIn campaigns are paused, charting daily clicks for a Pinterest campaign.*
- [Autron](https://autron.ai) **`C`** - Analyze and act on your Amazon advertising data. *Use case: Pausing an underperforming keyword bid, checking which product ad drove the most sales this week, adjusting budget on a campaign that's overspending.*

- [SearchTheTrend](https://www.searchthetrend.com) **`C`** - Tracks Meta advertisers' active ads, estimated spend, audience reach, ad rank, landing pages, and creative hooks, plus trending products by ad growth. *Use case: Pulling a competitor's top-spending ads, checking which age groups an ad actually reached, scanning a niche for products gaining ad volume.*
- [Sellantica](https://sellantica.ai) **`C`** - Reads Amazon Ads campaigns, keywords, search terms, bids, optimization suggestions, profit and inventory planning from Sellantica, and prepares, approves and executes changes to campaigns, bids, keywords, negatives, ad groups and product ads. *Use case: Harvesting converting search terms into new keywords, adding negatives to cut wasted spend, pausing an unprofitable campaign.*
- [SellerMate.AI — Amazon Ads & Retail Analytics](https://www.sellermate.ai) **`C`** - Analyzes Amazon Ads and Seller or Vendor Central retail data and updates campaign budgets and bids within account permission and policy limits. *Use case: Reviewing ACOS by campaign after a price change, finding search terms worth adding as negatives, raising a keyword bid within preset limits.*
- [Sgewise](https://segwise.ai) **`C`** - Queries advertising creative and campaign performance data, linking creative attributes such as hooks, CTAs, messaging, visuals and formats to metrics, and identifies winning patterns and creative fatigue. *Use case: Finding why performance dropped on a mobile game campaign last week, comparing hooks across top and bottom video ads, spotting creatives showing fatigue.*
- [Sherlok](https://sherlok.ai) **`C`** - Surfaces prioritized ad and analytics decisions with supporting evidence from connected sources, shows how past actions performed, investigates questions about spend and campaign performance, sends findings to a decision queue, approves, adjusts, discards or marks decisions applied within Sherlok, manages rules, goals and strategy, and runs, pauses or resumes its agents, without changing ad platforms. *Use case: Asking why paid search spend rose last week, approving today's top budget decision, checking how past applied decisions performed.*
- [Spotsnow](https://spotsnow.io) **`C`** - Podcast advertising and market intelligence. *Use case: Checking which podcasts a competitor advertised on this month as part of a market intelligence review.*
- [Stora: ASO & Apple Ads](https://stora.rocks) **`C`** - Researches App Store and Google Play keywords, competitor gaps and rank changes, drafts localized listing text, tracks experiments, and reviews imported Apple Ads spend, search terms and impression share for an app. *Use case: Finding keywords a competitor ranks for that an app misses, drafting a German subtitle for review, checking which Apple Ads search terms take the most spend.*
- [StoreAdOps](https://app.storeadops.ai) **`C`** - Reads advertising performance and Shopify commerce data across Google Ads, Meta, Microsoft Advertising, TikTok and OpenAI Ads, and prepares exact change previews that execute only on confirmation, with new advertising objects left paused. *Use case: Comparing Meta and Google spend against Shopify orders last week, previewing a budget cut on an underperforming campaign, setting up a new paused campaign for review.*
- [StreamSpend](https://mcp.streamspend.net) **`C`** - Compares a music ad campaign's price per result, streams, streams per click and saves against ranges from past MeansMGMT campaigns using screenshots, exports or typed numbers, and scores up to 25 of an artist's Spotify releases to plan which songs to promote next, with campaign type, country tier and reason. *Use case: Checking a single's Ads Manager export against past campaign ranges, choosing which of an artist's songs to push next.*
- [Supply Finder](https://supply-finder.com) **`C`** - Generates ranked programmatic site lists from a plain-language brief, checks campaign pacing, diagnoses deals that are not filling, flags underperforming domains, and queries campaign and deal delivery metrics. *Use case: Building a site list for a family travel brand's video campaign, finding why a private deal stopped filling, spotting domains dragging down viewability.*
- [Swipekit](https://swipekit.app) **`C`** - Searches a saved ad library and tracked-brand ads, previews image, video, and carousel creatives, and organizes ads into boards, folders, and tags. *Use case: Pulling competitor video ads for a campaign brief, saving strong hooks to a swipe board, retagging a folder of carousel ads.*
- [Targenix](https://targenix.uz) **`C`** - Reports Facebook and Instagram lead-form leads, partner order statuses and payouts, campaign profit, daily spend and revenue, and automation status in read-only mode with masked phone numbers. *Use case: Counting today's leads by campaign, finding which campaigns lost money yesterday, checking which automations failed.*
- [TikTok for Business](https://www.tiktok.com) - Manages TikTok advertising campaigns, audiences, and creative assets, and pulls performance reporting from the TikTok Ads platform. *Use case: Pausing an underperforming ad group, building an audience for a product launch, pulling last month's spend and conversion report.*
- [Tofu Ads](https://tofuads.com) **`C`** - Generates and revises on-brand LinkedIn single-image ads, LinkedIn carousels, and multi-size Google Display banners from a landing page, website, or creative brief. *Use case: Building a LinkedIn carousel from a product page, generating every Google Display size for one campaign, revising headline copy after a brand review.*
- [TrafficStars](https://trafficstars.com) **`C`** - Reports on ad network account performance and manages campaigns, creatives, bids, targeting, optimizer strategies, and publisher inventory. *Use case: Adjusting bids on an underperforming popunder campaign, blacklisting low-quality spots, reviewing publisher site inventory.*
- [Trendtrack](https://www.trendtrack.io) **`C`** - Searches Shopify stores and ads across Meta, Google and TikTok, finds similar and scaling brands, breaks down creatives, landing pages and emails, tracks brands with daily change reports, saves ads to boards, and writes competitor briefs. *Use case: Finding scaling skincare brands running new Meta ads, pulling a competitor brief before a pitch, saving winning ads to a creative board.*
- [TrueClicks MCP](https://trueclicks.com) **`C`** - Read-only access to TrueClicks PPC audits, alerts, budget pacing, tasks, and campaign, keyword, and search-term performance across Google, Microsoft, and Meta Ads. *Use case: Ranking audit issues by wasted spend before a client call, explaining why an account score dropped, flagging accounts pacing over budget this month.*
- [Tyton](https://usetyton.com) **`C`** - Audits a website's Meta Pixel and Conversions API setup, and for account holders saves a Conversions API token, proposes and approves conversion plans, installs server-side tracking and verifies the live setup. *Use case: Auditing a store's Meta Pixel for missing purchase events, approving a conversion plan, verifying that server events now arrive.*
- [Upspring](https://upspring.ai) **`C`** - Ad creative intelligence — yours and your market's. *Use case: Comparing a brand's ad creative to what others in its market are running.*
- [URLgenius](https://app.urlgeni.us) **`C`** - Creates, reads, lists and updates branded app deep links and QR codes for owned apps or supported third-party destinations, with custom paths and campaign parameters. *Use case: Creating a deep link that opens an Amazon product in the app with a campaign tag, generating a QR code for a print ad.*
- [Vazante](https://vazante.app) **`C`** - Reports spend, clicks, conversions, and cost metrics across Google, Meta, and TikTok ad accounts plus organic Facebook and Instagram, and can pause or resume campaigns. *Use case: Comparing cost per conversion across platforms, breaking down Meta results by placement, pausing an overspending campaign.*
- [Voluum](https://voluum.com) - Manage and analyze your Voluum ad-tracking campaigns directly from Claude. *Use case: Pausing a campaign that is underperforming, reviewing this week's results before a budget meeting.*
- [WASK - Facebook & Google Ads](https://www.wask.co) **`C`** - Reports Google Ads and Meta Ads performance by account, campaign, ad group, ad, keyword, search term and creative, compares periods, identifies top and bottom performers, and previews and applies actions on campaigns, ad groups, ads and keywords. *Use case: Comparing last week's ROAS against the prior week, finding underperforming keywords and pausing them after a preview.*
- [Watching That](https://watchingthat.com) **`C`** - Ad stack data covering yield insights, revenue performance, and delivery issues. *Use case: Monitoring video ad revenue, troubleshooting fill-rate issues, tracking yield performance.*
- [Wexlyn](https://wexlyn.ai) **`C`** - Reports ad cost, revenue, profit and ROI by source, campaign or ad under several attribution models, reads lead journeys, sales and calls, and records sales, calls, leads and lead stages. *Use case: Finding which ad sets made money last month, reading a lead's full journey, recording a closed sale from a call.*
- [Windsor.ai for Google Ads](https://windsor.ai/connectors/google-ads) **`C`** - Connect Google Ads to Claude: query ad spend, ROAS, CPC, conversions, and keyword performance in plain language, then create or pause campaigns, adjust budgets, and manage keywords. *Use case: Pausing a campaign that is overspending, raising the budget on one with strong ROAS, pausing a keyword with high CPC and no conversions.*
- [Windsor.ai for LinkedIn Ads](https://windsor.ai/connectors/linkedin-ads) **`C`** - Connect LinkedIn Ads to Claude: query ad spend, impressions, clicks, leads, and conversions in plain language, then pause or enable campaign groups and campaigns or set budgets. *Use case: Pausing a campaign group that stopped generating leads, checking cost per conversion before increasing a budget, reviewing clicks on a new ad.*
- [Windsor.ai for Microsoft Ads (Bing)](https://windsor.ai/connectors/bing-ads) **`C`** - Connect Microsoft Ads (Bing) to Claude: query ad spend, impressions, clicks, conversions, and CPC in plain language, then pause or enable campaigns and ad groups or set budgets. *Use case: Pausing an ad group with a high CPC and no conversions, raising the budget on a campaign that is converting well, checking impressions before a launch.*
- [Windsor.ai for TikTok Ads](https://windsor.ai/connectors/tiktok-ads) **`C`** - Connect TikTok Ads to Claude: query ad spend, ROAS, conversions, and campaign performance in plain language, then pause campaigns or adjust budgets. *Use case: Pausing a campaign whose ROAS dropped overnight, checking conversions on a new video ad, adjusting a budget before the weekend.*
- [Yirla](https://www.yirla.com) **`C`** - Competitor ad intelligence and campaign analytics for LinkedIn, Google, YouTube, Meta, Reddit, and TikTok ads. *Use case: Checking which of a competitor's TikTok ads has run longest, comparing their LinkedIn campaign volume against last quarter, pulling their top-performing Meta creative.*
- [Yolodex](https://www.yolodex.ai) **`C`** - Creative intelligence for teams running Meta ads. *Use case: Checking which Meta ad creative a competitor has been running the longest before launching a new campaign.*

## Brand Management and Content Creation

- [Abency](https://abency.com) - Gestiona todas las marcas de tu agencia desde Claude. *Use case: Revisando el estado de las campañas de una marca antes de una reunión con el cliente, comparando el rendimiento de dos marcas dentro de la cartera de la agencia.*
- [aclipp](https://www.aclipp.com) **`C`** - Analyzes media coverage, reach and media value by period, outlet, channel, country and sentiment, tracks brand share of voice and cited sources in AI answers, compares competitors, adds articles by URL, and builds PR reports and dashboards. *Use case: Measuring last quarter's coverage reach for a product launch, comparing a client's AI-answer share of voice with two competitors, adding a new article to a project before a monthly report.*
- [AgentFabrica](https://agentfabrica.com) **`C`** - Reads a marketing operations panel's brands and tone of voice, content calendar, pending approvals, ad pre-test results, lead counts, WhatsApp line status, plan quota and generated content, and adds draft posts to a brand's approval queue without publishing, approving or deleting anything. *Use case: Reviewing which posts are waiting for approval this week, summarizing objections from the latest ad pre-test, queuing a draft product-launch post for the team to approve.*
- [Agility PR Solutions](https://www.agilitypr.com) **`C`** - Searches media coverage across online, print, broadcast, podcast, radio, and social sources, with sentiment, share of voice, and coverage trends. *Use case: Summarizing coverage of a product announcement, comparing sentiment against a competitor over a quarter, charting share of voice through a campaign window.*
- [appfollow](https://appfollow.io) **`C`** - Connect AppFollow to your AI assistant via MCP. *Use case: Pulling data from a connected service into a conversation for a quick lookup, instead of switching to a separate app.*
- [AtmoScreen](https://atmoscreen.com) **`C`** - Lists waiting-room and lobby screens and their status, reads and creates slide programmes with weather, messages, QR codes and quizzes, and assigns programmes to one or more screens. *Use case: Creating an October waiting-room programme with local weather and a flu vaccination reminder, putting a new welcome loop on the reception screen, checking which screens are offline.*
- [Audesti - Voice testimonials](https://audesti.com) **`C`** - Reads voice testimonials with transcripts, sentiment and quotable phrases, creates testimonial requests and share links, approves and updates testimonials, and manages a share queue. *Use case: Pulling power phrases for a landing page, requesting testimonials from five recent customers, lining up the strongest testimonial to post.*
- [Black Ice](https://black-ice.ai) **`C`** - Looks up approved terminology per locale with allowed and forbidden variants, scans draft translations for terminology violations, returns market profiles with tone and locale guidance, checks market availability, browses the concept ontology and submits new term candidates to a human review queue. *Use case: Validating a Japanese product-page translation against approved terms, checking whether a feature is available in Brazil, proposing a new glossary term for review.*
- [BrandPilot](https://brandpilot.alvinsclub.ai) **`C`** - Reads a brand workspace's brief, connected accounts, content plan and draft posts for X, LinkedIn, TikTok, YouTube Shorts, Instagram and Hashnode, saves or updates the brand brief, creates content plans, edits draft posts and reads brand reports. *Use case: Updating a brand brief after a product pivot, generating next month's content plan, tightening the copy on a draft LinkedIn post.*
- [BrindesBrasil.com.br](https://www.brindesbrasil.com.br) **`C`** - Searches a Brazilian catalog of custom-branded promotional products by text, SKU or category, shows colors, imprint methods and quantity price tiers, calculates BRL unit and total prices by quantity and imprint count, and suggests similar products and other colors. *Use case: Pricing 500 branded tumblers with two imprints for a trade show, finding similar backpacks in another color, comparing imprint methods for a pen order.*
- [Brix](https://brixsignage.com) **`C`** - Finds digital signage screens and diagnoses why one is offline or blank, puts media and playlists on screens for a set time, posts quick announcements, and pulls proof-of-play, outage and uptime reports. *Use case: Figuring out why a lobby screen went blank this morning, showing a lunch special playlist on every menu board until 2pm, pulling proof-of-play for a retail window campaign.*
- [ChatifyIT](https://www.chatifyit.com) **`C`** - Reads ChatifyIT brand details, page link and recent posts, then saves a LinkedIn post and a matching list email as drafts in the ChatifyIT dashboard, with optional video or image attachments. *Use case: Drafting a LinkedIn post and email announcing a newly launched course, attaching a demo video to the draft, writing up a lesson learned from a client project.*
- [Checkback Restaurant Review](https://www.checkbackhq.com) **`C`** - Writes replies to restaurant Google and Yelp reviews, answering Spanish reviews in Spanish, and scores a restaurant's public Google Business Profile out of 100 with what is working and what to fix. *Use case: Answering a one-star complaint about slow service, thanking a regular for a five-star review, auditing a coffee shop's listing for a missing menu link.*
- [Checksocials](https://www.checksocials.com) **`C`** - Reads a workspace's company context, content rules, blog articles and social posts, proposes context updates that take effect only after approval in the app, and marks blog posts as published. *Use case: Pulling brand positioning before drafting a social post, repurposing a blog article into a social thread, proposing an updated competitor list.*

- [ClientGen Reach](https://clientgen.ai) **`C`** - Drafts postcards, handwritten notes and hosted personal pages for financial advisors from approved templates with proofs and prices, runs an automated SEC and FINRA pre-check, manages recipients, brand briefs and kits, submits drafts for compliance review and approves or cancels orders, which are placed only via an emailed confirmation link. *Use case: Drafting a birthday postcard campaign for a client list, routing it to the firm's compliance reviewer, building a seminar RSVP page.*

- [Plainpaper](https://plainpaper.io) **`C`** - Stores marketing work as linked research, audience, plan, email, and creative cards on a shared canvas with versions, comments, and approval gates. *Use case: Rebuilding campaign context in a fresh conversation, drafting an email against stored brand guidelines, staging creative for approval before anything ships.*
- [Pomo](https://usepomo.ai) **`C`** - Reads campaign performance, social listening, competitor ads, sales and web traffic, and drafts paid Meta and Google campaigns, social posts, articles, PR stories and outreach emails, with nothing launched or spent until confirmed. *Use case: Checking which Meta campaign drove the most sales last week, drafting a press pitch for a product launch, reviewing a competitor's new ads before planning a promo.*
- [Prelight](https://prelight.ai) **`C`** - Searches an organization's brand record of media assets, brand guidance, rights and inspiration documents by keyword, lists brands, and reads files, document content and asset thumbnails. *Use case: Checking brand usage rules before drafting a campaign, finding product imagery for a social post, confirming usage rights on a licensed photo.*
- [Pressmaster.ai](https://www.pressmaster.ai) **`C`** - Writes social posts, articles, newsletters, and video scripts against a trained voice profile and brand guide, publishes or schedules them, and reports engagement. *Use case: Running a content interview about a launch, scheduling the resulting LinkedIn post for Monday, pulling impressions and top posts for last quarter.*
- [Prisma Reviews](https://prismareviews.com) **`C`** - Lists reviews from the App Store, Google Play, Judge.me, eBay, Yelp, Tripadvisor, Booking.com and other platforms in one format, filters them by location, platform, date or reply status, reports average rating, rating distribution and reply rate, and publishes approved reply text through platform APIs where available. *Use case: Pulling this week's unanswered 1- and 2-star reviews across locations, summarizing what guests complain about on Tripadvisor, posting an approved reply to a Google Play review.*
- [Productised](https://www.productised.ai) - Create assessments and lead magnets. *Use case: Building a lead-diagnostic quiz, generating a personalized readiness scorecard, producing a branded outcome report for a prospect.*

- [Quotient](https://www.getquotient.ai) **`C`** - Plans and executes marketing campaigns across email, social, blog, and events, producing briefs, tasks, timelines, and the deliverables themselves. *Use case: Building a feature-launch campaign from a one-line goal, drafting the social posts for each channel, scheduling a webinar's supporting email sequence.*
- [Reachype](https://reachype.com) **`C`** - Reads a marketing workspace's brand profile, content calendar, idea backlog, campaigns, products, research reports and performance dashboards, and rewrites the copy of planned posts or ideas with undo. *Use case: Reviewing next week's content calendar against the brand voice, rewriting a planned post's caption, checking which channels performed well last month.*

- [Styleweb Vision](https://vision.styleweb.com.br) **`C`** - Reports marketplace sales, orders, shipping, listings, stock, prices, buyer questions and complaints through a Mercado Livre integration, reads CRM customers, price catalogs, price links and quotes and AI-training Q&A bases, and prepares stock and price changes, listing publications, buyer replies and quote responses that run only after confirmation. *Use case: Checking last week's Mercado Livre sales and pending shipments, answering a buyer's question on a listing, approving a received quote.*
- [Suger](https://www.suger.io) **`C`** - Manage your AWS, Azure, and GCP Marketplace business from Claude — private offers, buyers, entitlements, usage-based billing, revenue reporting, and multi-cloud co-sell. *Use case: Creating a private offer for a specific AWS Marketplace buyer, checking usage-based billing totals before a renewal, pulling a revenue report across all three clouds for a co-sell review.*
- [Sumtracker Inventory Manager](https://www.sumtracker.com) **`C`** - Shows available, booked and incoming stock per product and warehouse for sellers on Shopify, Amazon, Walmart, eBay and other channels, with low-stock and overstock items, stockout dates and reorder suggestions, looks up sales orders and fulfillment lines, creates and updates warehouses, addresses and access, manages channel inventory sync and error logs, and guides Shopify location mapping and product syncing. *Use case: Listing products that will stock out within two weeks, turning off inventory sync on an eBay channel, reading why an Amazon listing failed to update.*
- [SuperProfile](https://superprofile.bio) **`C`** - Creates, edits, publishes and analyzes digital products, payment pages, courses, locked content, lead magnets and webinars, manages product emails and bump offers, and drafts and runs Instagram and Messenger DM automations. *Use case: Drafting a comment-to-DM automation for a new Instagram reel, publishing a mini-course with a bump offer, pulling last month's webinar attendance and sales.*
- [Sweeprice](https://www.sweeprice.com) **`C`** - Reads repricing data for sellers on Amazon, Cdiscount, Fnac, Darty, ManoMano, eBay, Leroy Merlin, Boulanger, BUT, Conforama, La Redoute, Back Market, Etsy and Decathlon, including price positions against the top competing offer, which seller holds that offer, price history, recommended prices and alerts, and adds tracked products, sets floor and ceiling prices, chooses pricing modes and marks notifications read. *Use case: Finding products priced above the top competing offer on Cdiscount, reviewing last night's ranked alerts, setting a floor and ceiling price for a newly tracked product.*
- [Syndic8](https://syndic8.io) **`C`** - Finds products missing retailer-required attributes, runs channel readiness preflight checks, matches valid values, queries inventory, pricing and purchase orders, and creates products, bulk-updates fields, manages collections and attaches media. *Use case: Listing products missing required Home Depot attributes before an export, bulk-updating a color field across a furniture line, checking which items fail a Walmart preflight.*
- [Tapcart](https://www.tapcart.com) **`C`** - Analyzes Tapcart mobile app performance for Shopify brands, schedules push campaigns, manages customer segments, and builds App Studio screen layouts. *Use case: Comparing install and revenue trends after a homepage change, scheduling a weekend push to a lapsed-buyer segment, sending a test notification first.*
- [TendMyStore for WooCommerce](https://tendmystore.com) **`C`** - Manages a WooCommerce store, creating, updating and deleting products, variations, orders, coupons, customers, shipping and tax settings, importing and exporting data, recording refunds and reading sales reports. *Use case: Updating stock for a product variation, creating a 15 percent coupon, generating a packing slip for an order.*
- [Tiana & Co. Seller Compliance](https://www.tiana-co.com) **`C`** - Answers US product compliance questions for marketplace sellers, covering required certificates such as CPC, GCC, FCC and FDA import steps, marketplace documentation asks, held shipments, rule changes and supplier screening against US forced-labor and sanctions lists. *Use case: Finding which certificate a children's toy needs before listing on Amazon, screening a new supplier against the UFLPA Entity List, working out why a shipment was held.*
- [Tiendanube](https://www.tiendanube.com) **`C`** - Creá, gestioná, analizá y automatizá tu Tiendanube. *Use case: Creando un nuevo producto antes de una promoción, revisando las ventas de la semana, automatizando el envío de un descuento a clientes inactivos.*
- [TomeScout](https://tomescout.com) **`C`** - Packs keyword phrases into the seven KDP keyword boxes, checks book listings against KDP metadata guidelines, calculates eBook, paperback and Kindle Unlimited royalties across 13 Amazon stores, and converts Amazon sales rank into daily and monthly sales ranges. *Use case: Filling keyword boxes for a cozy mystery before launch, checking what a 4.99 dollar ebook earns in the UK store, estimating monthly sales for a competitor ranked 5,000.*
- [Tool4seller](https://www.tool4seller.com) **`C`** - Connect Claude to your Amazon seller data for sales, profit, inventory, advertising, traffic, reviews, and FBA inbound insights. *Use case: Checking profit margin on a specific SKU after a price change, comparing advertising spend against sales for a product line, reviewing FBA inbound shipment status.*
- [Tourist.com Partners](https://partners.tourist.com/connect-ai) **`C`** - Creates and updates business listings and percentage discount offers on a global traveler deals catalog, including Plus-only offers and promo codes, pauses or deletes offers, adds photos, logos and offer images, and reports 30-day page views, saves, clicks, traffic sources and city ranking, with a preview before each change. *Use case: Launching a 15% lunch discount for a cafe, filling in missing opening hours and a booking link on a hotel listing, checking the last 30 days of page views and city ranking.*
- [TradeTrack TCG](https://www.tradetracktcg.com) **`C`** - Manages trading card game inventory for vendors and shops, searching cards and market prices, adding, updating and deleting inventory items, recording sales and trades, reversing transactions and managing staff invites. *Use case: Adding a box of new singles to inventory, recording a trade at a card show, checking market prices before repricing stock.*
- [tseller.co.za](https://tseller.co.za) **`C`** - Reads Takealot seller sales, orders, stock, fees, payouts, returns, Buy Box position, and reviews, and edits listing prices, stock, and availability. *Use case: Comparing last month's fees against payouts, raising the price on a listing losing the Buy Box, checking which items need replenishment before a sale.*
- [Tusella](https://tusella.com) **`C`** - Reads Etsy shop listings and unpublished changes, edits titles, tags, descriptions, prices and photos on one or many listings as drafts, creates or copies listings, reverts edits, and prepares publish reviews. *Use case: Rewriting tags on twenty mug listings for the holiday season, raising prices across a print collection by 10 percent, comparing a draft listing with what is live before publishing.*
- [Underpriced AI](https://underpricedai.com) **`C`** - Estimates resale value from recent eBay sold listings, eBay US in USD by default, returning a price range, comparables, three list prices with sell-through odds, net after fees and a buy-or-pass verdict, and prepares eBay listing drafts for review. *Use case: Checking what a vintage Pyrex bowl sells for, deciding whether a $15 thrift-store price is worth paying.*
- [Valigara](https://valigara.com) **`C`** - Searches and updates a jeweler's multichannel catalog of products, gemstones and stock by location, lists and updates orders and tracking, checks live and failed listings on marketplaces such as Amazon, eBay, Etsy, Walmart and Shopify, pushes price and quantity changes, imports and exports products, and runs and monitors batch jobs and saved automation procedures. *Use case: Finding unshipped Etsy orders that still need tracking, pushing a price change on a diamond ring to every marketplace, reading the errors from a failed eBay posting batch.*
- [Vendooly](https://vendooly.com) **`C`** - Query your Amazon Seller and Ads data in natural language and prepare actions on prices, stock and campaigns. *Use case: Checking why a listing's price changed overnight, pausing a campaign that is overspending, confirming a stock update before it goes live.*
- [Vori MCP](https://vori.com) **`C`** - Looks up and updates products, departments and prices for independent grocery stores, builds and publishes promotions, drafts purchase orders, and checks inventory, invoices, transactions, electronic shelf labels and sales analytics. *Use case: Drafting a purchase order for a produce vendor, publishing a weekend promotion on dairy items, counting out-of-stock products before a delivery arrives.*
- [Whatalo](https://whatalo.com) **`C`** - Browses a connected store's products, categories, customers, orders and inventory, creates and updates products and categories, manages product images, adjusts stock and updates order status. *Use case: Checking which orders are still pending this week, adding a new product with photos to a category, correcting stock levels after a warehouse recount.*
- [Which Sensor](https://www.whichsensor.com) **`C`** - Searches industrial sensors by technical attributes such as measuring range, switching distance or output, looks up part numbers, returns datasheets, list prices and company-discounted prices, finds replacements for discontinued sensors, and files quote requests after approval. *Use case: Finding an inductive proximity sensor with an 8 mm switching distance, locating a replacement for a discontinued part number, requesting a quote for 20 units.*

- [Zendrop](https://www.zendrop.com) **`C`** - Manage your dropshipping business in Claude. *Use case: Checking which product is running low on stock, reviewing recent order status before restocking.*
- [Zentor](https://www.zentor.io) **`C`** - Reads Zentor pricing, promotion, and advertising data for Mercado Libre listings, including costs, prices, margins, sales, and profitability alerts per listing. *Use case: Checking which listings sell below target margin, reviewing what a price change did to sales last week, triaging the open profitability alerts.*
- [Zineps](https://www.zineps.com) **`C`** - Creates, cancels, remakes and downloads shipping labels for webshop orders, sends labels to PrintNode, tracks shipments by track-and-trace code, lists shipping methods and parcel shops, and reads accounting invoices, usage analytics and support tickets on a logistics platform. *Use case: Creating a label for a Shopify order, finding where a parcel stalled using its tracking code, reprinting a damaged label.*

## Email SMS and Lifecycle Marketing

- [ActiveCampaign](https://www.activecampaign.com) - Marketing automation. *Use case: Email campaigns, lead scoring, CRM automation, customer journey mapping.*
- [Adflipr](https://adflipr.com) **`C`** - Searches email campaigns, automations, popups and landing pages, reads performance and cart-recovery summaries, creates campaigns, templates, popups and landing pages, manages sending-domain setup, schedules or sends campaigns, publishes popups and pauses or activates automations. *Use case: Reviewing abandoned-cart recovery for last month, drafting a holiday campaign from a template, scheduling it for Friday morning.*
- [Adobe Journey Optimizer](https://business.adobe.com/products/journey-optimizer.html) - Campaign and customer journey orchestration. *Use case: Cross-channel campaign troubleshooting, journey performance analysis, marketing automation diagnostics.*
- [Alia](https://www.aliapopups.com) **`C`** - Analyzes the performance of popups that collect email and SMS signups. *Use case: Comparing signup rates across popups, checking how many SMS subscribers a popup brought in, spotting a popup that stopped converting.*
- [Attentive](https://www.attentive.com) - Creates, schedules, pauses and resumes SMS, RCS, push and email campaigns, builds and publishes automated journeys, creates and sizes audience segments, edits email templates and brand styles, generates on-brand copy and short links, and queries campaign and journey metrics. *Use case: Building an automated SMS journey from a template, sizing an audience segment before a sale, pulling performance metrics for last week's campaigns.*
- [AWeber](https://www.aweber.com) **`C`** - Write newsletters, add and update subscribers, search email lists, and pull engagement performance stats, all without leaving the conversation. *Use case: Drafting a newsletter for a product launch, adding a batch of subscribers from a recent webinar, checking the open rate on last week's campaign before sending a follow-up.*
- [BayEngage](https://targetbay.com) **`C`** - Builds email campaigns, templates and audience segments from store customer data, schedules, pauses or cancels campaigns, sends test emails, and reports campaign and automation performance. *Use case: Building a segment of repeat buyers who have not ordered in 90 days, scheduling a weekend sale email, comparing performance across last month's campaigns.*
- [beehiiv](https://www.beehiiv.com) - Connects a beehiiv workspace covering posts, podcasts, automations, segments, products, and monetization data for drafting, design, and analysis. *Use case: Drafting a newsletter issue, checking which segment opens most, reviewing subscription revenue for the quarter.*
- [BlueConic](https://www.blueconic.com) - A customer data platform that unifies customer profiles and signals for real-time marketing activation. *Use case: Querying tenant customer-profile data to understand which segments are driving repeat purchases.*
- [Bouncer Email Verification MCP Server](https://www.usebouncer.com) **`C`** - Verifies single email addresses or batch lists with deliverability status and quality flags such as role-based or catch-all, checks domains, runs toxicity checks for breached or spam-trap addresses, and reports remaining credits. *Use case: Cleaning a 3,000-address list before Friday's newsletter send, checking why a prospect's email keeps bouncing, flagging role-based addresses in a trade-show lead list.*
- [Braze MCP Server](https://www.braze.com) - Access Braze REST API. *Use case: Customer engagement campaigns, push notification management, marketing automation.*
- [Brevo](https://www.brevo.com) - Marketing campaigns, audience insights, and drafts. *Use case: Email and SMS campaigns, audience segmentation, drafting and analyzing marketing sends.*
- [CampaignHQ](https://www.campaignhq.co) **`C`** - Reports email, WhatsApp and SMS campaign, automation and chat-flow results, diagnoses why contacts miss messages, drafts and tests campaigns and templates, builds segments, draft automations and chat flows, adds, updates, tags, unsubscribes and deletes contacts, queries events and funnels, and searches, assigns, closes and replies to inbox conversations. *Use case: Comparing last month's WhatsApp and email campaign revenue, diagnosing why a contact stopped getting SMS, drafting a win-back email for a lapsed segment.*
- [Carillon](https://carillon.dev) **`C`** - Configures app push credentials and API keys, debugs devices and tags, manages audiences, traces messages, and sends one-off or targeted push notification campaigns, including test sends. *Use case: Uploading push credentials for a new app, sending a test notification to one device, targeting a re-engagement push at a tagged user audience.*
- [channel](https://askcuri.com/en/docs/claude) **`C`** - Reads topics, email campaigns, subscriber stats, posts, trend reports and creators in Curi’s channel workspace, and creates or edits drafts of topics, campaigns and posts without sending. *Use case: Drafting a newsletter campaign around a new trend report, checking subscriber counts before planning a launch email, outlining next month's topics.*
- [Churn Solution](https://churnsolution.com) **`C`** - Churn prevention and customer retention tools. *Use case: Spotting which customers are at risk of canceling this month, checking which retention offer worked best last quarter.*

- [ClickTimer](https://www.clicktimer.co) **`C`** - Creates, updates, deletes and lists email countdown timers, returns embed code, reports impressions and credits, and with a connected Klaviyo account lists templates, publishes timers as content blocks and sends test emails. *Use case: Building a countdown for a Black Friday sale email, changing a timer's end date and colors, publishing a timer to Klaviyo for an abandoned-cart flow.*
- [Commune](https://usecommune.com) **`C`** - Reports newsletter article performance, subscriber sources and reader engagement, reads community conversations and highlights, and drafts, revises, test-sends, schedules and sends articles to subscribers. *Use case: Checking how last Tuesday's article did against the newsletter overall, finding readers who are drifting away, sending a test copy before scheduling the next issue.*

- [Email Verify](https://email-verify.simplebusinesssuite.com) **`C`** - Checks whether email addresses are real and safe to send to using syntax validation, disposable-domain detection, MX records and live SMTP verification, returning a valid, invalid, risky or unknown verdict with the reason for each address. *Use case: Cleaning a pasted list of 200 newsletter signups before an import, checking whether a sales lead's address is deliverable, flagging disposable addresses from a signup form.*

- [Emailr](https://emailr.dev) **`C`** - Reads an email workspace profile, configured sending domains, saved templates and broadcast campaign statuses in read-only mode, without sending, scheduling or editing anything. *Use case: Confirming a sending domain is configured before a launch, summarizing which campaigns are still drafts, checking which templates already exist for an onboarding sequence.*
- [EmailVerify](https://emailverify.techforce.global) **`C`** - Verifies single email addresses or batches of up to 50, checking syntax, domain and MX records, disposable and role-based addresses, live SMTP mailboxes, catch-all domains, SPF and DMARC and common domain typos, and returns a status, a 0-100 confidence score and reasons. *Use case: Cleaning a 50-contact lead list before an outreach campaign, catching a typo in a customer's email domain, flagging role-based addresses before a CRM import.*

## Loyalty Referrals and Partner Programs

- [Affil](https://www.affil.ai) **`C`** - Reads product terms, compliance guidelines and mockup review feedback from an Affil account, provides a compliance review guide for checking drafts, and reports link clicks and conversions over the last 7, 30 or 90 days. *Use case: Checking a draft affiliate post against brand guidelines, finding out what reviewers want changed in a mockup, comparing link conversions over the last 30 days.*

- [Bot do Afiliado](https://botdoafiliado.com) **`C`** - Converts product links into affiliate links for Brazilian affiliates on Shopee, Mercado Livre, Amazon, AliExpress, Magalu and networks such as AWIN, searches products with prices, builds Telegram and WhatsApp offer posts from bot templates, manages coupons and message templates, configures and tests channel-reposting rules and reads WhatsApp campaign, group and activity metrics. *Use case: Turning a batch of Shopee product links into affiliate links, building a WhatsApp offer post for a discounted air fryer, adding a new Mercado Livre coupon to the bot.*

- [UE Advocacy](https://userevidence.com) **`C`** - Works with a UserEvidence customer advocacy program: finds reference customers by industry, size or use case, shows which open opportunities have advocate support, tracks advocate engagement, reference requests and how often each customer has been asked, browses the rewards catalog, and lists campaign missions and participants. *Use case: Finding a reference customer of similar size for a late-stage deal, checking how often an advocate has been asked this quarter, listing stalled reference requests and their owners.*
- [Unifyr](https://www.unifyr.com) **`C`** - The agentic Partner Relationship Management (PRM) and Channel Sales and Marketing (TCMA) platform for indirect channel sales. *Use case: Checking which channel partner is closing the most indirect deals this quarter, coordinating a joint marketing campaign with a reseller.*
- [Vuelvy](https://vuelvy.com) **`C`** - Reads business profiles, Apple Wallet and Google Wallet programs, audiences, automations, benefits and campaigns, plus email campaigns, flows, segments and templates, previews wallet campaigns and creates wallet programs or saves drafts after an exact preview and explicit approval. *Use case: Reviewing which Apple Wallet campaigns ran for a coffee shop last month, previewing a Google Wallet campaign before approval, drafting a new wallet program for a second location.*
- [Wallet Pass Builder](https://walletpassbuilder.com) **`C`** - Creates, updates and archives Apple Wallet and Google Wallet passes, runs loyalty stamp cards, records loyalty visits, lists customers and sends or cancels lock-screen notifications. *Use case: Creating a membership card for a gym, adding a stamp for a coffee customer, sending a notification about a weekend offer.*
- [Walletap](https://www.walletap.io) **`C`** - Design a wallet pass template, issue passes against it, and push updates to phones that already hold one - loyalty cards, tickets and memberships across Apple and Google Wallet. *Use case: Designing a template for a new loyalty card, issuing tickets for an event, pushing an update to everyone who already has one on their phone.*
- [YourPartnerOS](https://www.yourpartneros.com) **`C`** - Explains ISV partner program rules, returns weekly partner news, runs a practice maturity check, and with an account shows partner health, deal registration status, account overlaps, meeting prep and draft notes or messages held for review. *Use case: Checking deal registration status before a call, prepping for a partner QBR, finding which partners share a target account.*
- [Zeydoo](https://zeydoo.com) **`C`** - Reads Zeydoo CPA network offers, payout rates, account balance, and traffic statistics for affiliates and publishers. *Use case: Checking last week's earnings against the balance, breaking conversions down by country, finding catalogue offers that pay for Android traffic from Brazil.*

## Marketing Analytics and Consumer Insights

- [ABConvert](https://www.abconvert.io) **`C`** - Inspects and compares Shopify A/B test results, creates draft tests, previews storefront changes, and manages experiment lifecycles. *Use case: Checking whether a price test has a winner, drafting a checkout experiment, previewing a storefront variant before launch.*
- [Acoustic](https://www.acoustic.com) **`C`** - The performance edge for your marketing data - right in Claude. *Use case: Checking which marketing campaign performed best last month, spotting a dip in performance before it becomes a trend.*
- [Admetrics](https://www.admetrics.io) **`C`** - Exposes Admetrics Query Language analytics as validated tools that agents and coding assistants can call for marketing measurement. *Use case: Querying campaign performance for a date range, checking a metric definition before reporting it, pulling channel results into an analysis.*
- [Adobe CX Enterprise Coworker](https://business.adobe.com/products/cx-enterprise-coworker/ai-assistant.html) - Answers natural-language questions about customer experience insights, analyzes customer experiences and completes marketing tasks across Adobe CX Enterprise, with English-only support. *Use case: Asking which customer segments drove the most conversions last quarter, analyzing where customers drop off in an onboarding journey.*
- [Adobe Marketing Agent](https://business.adobe.com/blog/introducing-adobe-marketing-agent-microsoft-365-copilot) - Marketing campaign and audience insights from the Adobe stack. *Use case: Campaign performance analysis, audience segmentation, cross-product Adobe marketing diagnostics.*
- [Adzviser](https://adzviser.com) **`C`** - Talk to your marketing and sales data intuitively. *Use case: Asking which sales rep closed the most deals this quarter, checking why marketing spend spiked last week.*
- [AgencyAnalytics](https://agencyanalytics.com) **`C`** - Reports on client marketing performance across SEO, PPC, social, email, call tracking and ecommerce channels connected through AgencyAnalytics' 85+ integrations. *Use case: Pulling a month-end summary for one client's paid and organic channels, comparing two accounts' call volumes, checking which campaigns moved since the last report.*
- [AIlon](https://ailon.io/ailon-api-zielgruppen-insights-media-aktivierung) **`C`** - Generates audience definitions from natural-language descriptions and returns aggregated demographic and behavioral insights, comparing segments for German media planning and targeting. *Use case: Defining the audience for a premium product launch, comparing two campaign segments on media consumption, checking which attributes separate buyers from non-buyers.*
- [AI事業変革シミュレーター](https://feelflow.net) **`C`** - Reframes a business by audience, offering, and delivery, returning Japanese-language business ideas with estimated success probabilities, SWOT, personas, and action plans. *Use case: Running an analysis on a new service line, opening the customer journey behind a promising idea, revisiting an analysis run earlier on the web.*
- [api.mergn.com](https://www.mergn.com) **`C`** - Returns read-only performance reporting for MERGN email, SMS, push and WhatsApp campaigns and journeys, including sends, deliveries, views, clicks, conversions and revenue by date range and channel, along with the platform's own metric definitions. *Use case: Checking which channel drove the most revenue last quarter, comparing March campaign clicks against February, confirming how a conversion is defined.*
- [app.reportgarden.com](https://reportgarden.com) **`C`** - Queries agency clients' marketing data from advertising, analytics, SEO and ecommerce integrations such as Google Ads, Meta, Google Analytics 4 and Shopify, looks up clients, accounts, campaigns and budgets, and creates, updates, schedules, shares and emails reports. *Use case: Comparing month-over-month spend and ROAS for a client's Google and Meta campaigns, creating a monthly report from a template, scheduling it to email the client.*
- [Appfigures](https://appfigures.com) **`C`** - Searches apps with download and revenue estimates, demographics and SDKs, tracks ASO keyword ranks and suggestions, reads Apple Ads campaigns, keywords and reports, queries connected app metrics, and lists and replies to reviews. *Use case: Finding competitors' top organic keywords, tracking keyword rank trends for an app, replying to recent one-star reviews.*
- [AppGoat](https://appgoat.ai) **`C`** - Looks up App Store and Google Play apps with metadata, ratings, AI-tagged reviews, rating and install history, listing changes, charts and competitors, researches and tracks keywords and rankings, runs app audits with metadata rewrites, watches apps for changes, and proposes review replies, listing updates and Apple Ads bid changes that apply only after approval. *Use case: Auditing an app's listing and getting a rewritten subtitle within character limits, pulling crash-related reviews from the last month, comparing keyword rankings against two competitors.*
- [Appier Enterprise MCP Server](https://www.appier.com/en) **`C`** - Provides Appier Enterprise customer insights, campaign and journey performance, audiences, and marketing content for teams using AIQUA and AIRIS. *Use case: Reviewing how a cross-channel journey performed, checking which audience segment converted best, pulling campaign content for a planning review.*
- [AppmanAI](https://appmanai.com) **`C`** - Returns App Store and Google Play data for app store optimization, including app metadata, ratings, category rankings, keyword popularity, difficulty and volatility, keyword rankings, competitor overlap and reviews, and drafts keyword lists and metadata. *Use case: Finding high-popularity, low-difficulty keywords for a fitness app, checking ranking history for a target keyword, drafting a new subtitle from store data.*
