- [Outward Intelligence](https://www.outwardintelligence.com) **`C`** - Answers business questions from an organization's brand tracking and ad hoc market research studies. *Use case: Checking brand awareness trends in the latest tracking wave, comparing results across audiences in an ad hoc study.*
- [Oviond](https://www.oviond.com) **`C`** - Build, update, and analyze client marketing reports and dashboards across 100+ data sources, creating reports, querying live campaign data, and generating PDFs. *Use case: Pulling live campaign data from a specific channel into a client dashboard, generating a PDF report ahead of a monthly review, updating a dashboard after adding a new data source.*
- [Owlbert from The Institute](https://www.marketingscience.info) **`C`** - Answers marketing questions from Ehrenberg-Bass Institute research on the Laws of Growth, retrieving specific laws, benchmarks and background evidence, computing Dirichlet model figures, and flagging open research questions. *Use case: Checking whether a challenger brand should prioritize reach or loyalty, estimating repeat-purchase rates for a category with the Dirichlet model, pulling the evidence behind a law before a strategy review.*
- [pagent.ai](https://www.pagent.ai) **`C`** - Creates, launches, pauses, and analyzes website experiments in pagent.ai, generating copy, image, and layout variants for the pages under test. *Use case: Spinning up three headline variants for a pricing page, pausing a test that is losing conversions, reading results before rolling a winning layout out.*
- [Peec AI](https://peec.ai) - Brand visibility analytics across LLMs. *Use case: Tracking how brands appear in ChatGPT/Claude/Gemini answers, AI-search SEO, generative-engine optimization.*
- [Pendo](https://www.pendo.io) - Product and user insights. *Use case: Product analytics, in-app guidance, user onboarding, feature adoption tracking.*
- [Personyze](https://www.personyze.com) **`C`** - Creates and reviews website personalization campaigns, A/B tests, and product recommendations, with audience, goal, and traffic analytics from a Personyze account. *Use case: Drafting a welcome offer aimed at first-time visitors, checking how a pricing-headline test is splitting traffic, reviewing which recommendation row converts best.*
- [Polar Analytics](https://www.polaranalytics.com) - Shopify and DTC e-commerce analytics dashboard. *Use case: Centralizing Shopify, ad, and marketing data in one dashboard; tracking gross/net sales, retention, and profit metrics for DTC brands.*
- [Porter Metrics](https://portermetrics.com) **`C`** - Queries live campaign data across Meta Ads, Google Ads, TikTok, GA4, Shopify, and other platforms, and publishes hosted dashboards at shareable URLs. *Use case: Asking for spend by campaign week over week, publishing a client dashboard on a stable link, scheduling an export of a saved account blend.*
- [Potloc](https://www.potloc.com) **`C`** - Summarizes survey study reports with chart data, pulls raw and open-ended responses, breaks results down by segment, builds draft reports, edits questionnaires, and exchanges comments with the research team on wording. *Use case: Finding themes in open-ended answers about pricing, crossing a satisfaction question by region, adding a screener to the next wave's questionnaire.*
- [PreVibe](https://kometo.co) **`C`** - Runs live web research on a product idea and returns a scored market-research report with key findings, sourced figures, and failure risks. *Use case: Checking whether a SaaS idea has a market before building, reading the strongest angles behind a score, reviewing the reasons it could fail.*
- [Pricing Optimizer AI](https://pricingoptimizerai.com) **`C`** - Finds comparable public SaaS products and reports their plans, prices, vendor-described features, and observed price changes, each linked to the vendor page. *Use case: Benchmarking a new tier against three competitors, checking whether a rival raised prices this year, tracing a quoted price back to its vendor page.*
- [ProLevelMax](https://prolevelmax.com) **`C`** - Collects live public posts, comments, profiles, engagement and reviews from Reddit, TikTok, YouTube, Instagram, X, Amazon, Google Maps and 150+ other platforms, quoting a price before each paid run, and creates scheduled runs for brand monitoring. *Use case: Pulling this week's top r/SaaS posts and summarizing the complaints, grouping 1-to-3-star reviews of an Amazon product by issue, scheduling a weekly brand-mention collection.*
- [QR-hi](https://qr-hi.com) **`C`** - Creates, bulk-creates, re-points, pauses, deletes and lists dynamic QR codes and short links in a QR-hi account, fetches QR images in saved designs and reports scan statistics by period and country. *Use case: Creating one QR code per restaurant table, re-pointing a printed poster code to a new events page, checking which codes got the most scans.*
- [RAMMP](https://www.rammp.com) **`C`** - Diagnose why marketing isn't working. *Use case: Asking why a recent marketing effort underperformed and where to start looking.*
- [Rapisight](https://rapisight.com) **`C`** - Runs credit-estimated research apps over social and review data, including insight and trend discovery, product feedback, preference drivers, YouTube channel benchmarks, creator content analysis and KOL identification, and reads sourced reports, findings and collected posts, schedules recurring runs and shares reports. *Use case: Finding what owners complain about in a rival's app, ranking creators for a skincare campaign, scheduling a monthly trend scan.*
- [Rastro](https://rastropixel.com) **`C`** - Reports campaigns, Shopee sales, commissions, products, links, ROAS and daily results from a Rastro account, checks integration status and conversion API diagnostics, and can change Meta ad status or budget with extra scope and approval. *Use case: Comparing ROAS between this month and last, checking which Shopee products earned the most commission, diagnosing why conversions stopped reaching Meta.*
- [Recommend](https://www.recommend.studio) **`C`** - Reports campaign, promotion and sales-action results by brand, market, channel or period, suggests next actions from a predictive model, runs and reviews commercial workflows, and returns every figure with its traced source. *Use case: Comparing promotion results across two markets for the last quarter, asking which action to run next for a slow-selling brand, tracing a revenue figure back to its source before a review.*
- [Reporting Ninja](https://www.reportingninja.com) **`C`** - Queries and blends client marketing data from connected accounts such as Google Ads, Meta Ads, GA4, Search Console, LinkedIn, TikTok, Microsoft Ads, Shopify, HubSpot and Mailchimp, and, with write access, builds branded reports from templates or from scratch, schedules email delivery and publishes them to client portals. *Use case: Finding which campaigns spent without converting for a client, checking budget pacing across Google and Meta Ads, setting up a monthly branded report emailed to the client.*
- [REVIEWS.AI](https://www.reviews.ai) **`C`** - Reads aggregated customer reviews from monitored products and locations, returning sentiment summaries, ratings, NPS, star distributions, topics, extracts and review text, and exports filtered reviews to CSV. *Use case: Pulling this month's one-star reviews for a product, comparing complaint themes between two quarters, exporting Amazon reviews to CSV.*
- [Roivenue](https://www.roivenue.com) **`C`** - Answers questions about Roivenue attribution data — channel and campaign revenue, cost, ROMI, conversions, customer journeys — and reports data-source freshness, read-only. *Use case: Checking how paid search performed under a data-driven attribution model, tracing the touchpoints behind one order, confirming when a source last refreshed.*
- [Scanlo](https://scanlo.io) **`C`** - Creates tracked QR codes and multi-link hubs for small service businesses, repoints or switches off existing codes, and reports scan counts by code and placement and hub button taps. *Use case: Creating a Google reviews QR code for a front desk, repointing a window booking code to a new scheduler, comparing scans between counter and window placements.*
- [SegMetrics](https://segmetrics.io) **`C`** - Queries marketing attribution data, funnel conversion rates, contact segments, and top traffic sources across products, tags, lists, and campaigns. *Use case: Finding which traffic source drives the most revenue, comparing funnel conversion by campaign, segmenting leads by purchase history.*
- [Signal by StackEnhance](https://stackenhance.com) **`C`** - Reports how often ecommerce brands send marketing email, drawing on an archive of more than 1,000 online stores mostly in Sweden, Norway, Denmark, Finland and Germany, with brand summaries and links to their campaign pages. *Use case: Checking how many emails a competing fashion brand sent last month, finding which brands in Sweden email most often.*
- [Smacient](https://smacient.com/products/marketing-context-claude) **`C`** - Marketing data and competitive intelligence - analytics, ads, social, marketplaces, and app stores - straight into Claude. *Use case: Checking how a competitor's app store ranking moved after their last update, comparing social engagement against a rival brand, pulling marketplace pricing data before adjusting a listing.*
- [Snipzr](https://www.snipzr.com) **`C`** - Creates branded short links singly or up to 100 at once, builds UTM campaign links, generates QR codes with custom colors and a center logo, and reports daily clicks, QR scans and campaign rollups with bot traffic counted separately. *Use case: Shortening 40 event links on a branded domain for a conference, making a logo-centered QR code for a flyer, comparing clicks by campaign after a newsletter.*
- [Songstats](https://developers.stats.company/mcp) **`C`** - Returns read-only music analytics for artists, labels, collaborators and tracks, comparing performance over time, audience geography, playlist and curator drivers and release momentum across supported music platforms, with catalog search and Enterprise API endpoint access. *Use case: Comparing an artist's performance before and after a new single, finding which playlists drove a track's growth, mapping where a label's audience is concentrated.*
- [Source](https://source.app) **`C`** - Collects business data such as page visits, calendar bookings, sales funnel stages and payments under a single contact ID, answers questions and runs SQL over it, and lists tracked websites and describes the available data. *Use case: Tracing which first-visit pages led to paying customers, counting bookings that converted to payments last month.*
- [Strateego](https://strateego.ai) **`C`** - Reads competitive intelligence from Strateego, including competitors' top social posts, recent ads, new moves, brand positioning, Google reputation and semantic search across their content. *Use case: Reviewing competitors' latest Meta ads, comparing Google review ratings, summarizing a rival's new product moves this month.*
- [Supercurve Marketing Teammate](https://supercurve.ai) **`C`** - Reads paid ad performance across Google, Meta, LinkedIn, Bing and ChatGPT Ads, competitor research, AI-search visibility, analytics, knowledge base, inbox and meeting notes from Supercurve, tracks competitors and AI-search prompts, sets LinkedIn content pillars and drafts posts and blog articles for approval. *Use case: Checking which prompts mention a brand in AI answers, adding a new competitor to tracking, drafting a LinkedIn post from a short brief.*
- [Tellz](https://tellz.ai) **`C`** - Delivers a daily brief of competitors' public Instagram posts, Facebook and Instagram ads, LinkedIn posts, news mentions and watched web pages, searches collected items, and proposes, confirms or undoes changes to followed competitors, sources and brief timing. *Use case: Pulling today's competitor brief, searching every offer a rival posted this month, adding a news source to watch.*
- [Tesemble](https://tesemble.com) **`C`** - Tests product briefs and outbound copy against a factorial grid of synthetic buyer personas, suggesting audiences and message variants, running ICP discovery, GTM validation and message hunts, comparing and forking runs, saving audiences and reading segment insights, with simulations spending account credits. *Use case: Testing three cold-email openers against synthetic CFO personas, running ICP discovery for a new analytics product, comparing two simulation runs by segment.*
- [ThankYou Analytics](https://thankyouanalytics.com) **`C`** - Reads channel performance, attribution model comparisons, positional contribution, channel interactions and device and browser breakdowns from a ThankYou Analytics account. *Use case: Comparing how paid social is credited under different attribution models, finding which channels act as closers, reviewing ROAS by channel last quarter.*
- [ThoughtMetric](https://thoughtmetric.io) **`C`** - Queries e-commerce marketing attribution across channels for Shopify, WooCommerce, BigCommerce, Magento and custom stores, with five attribution models and configurable lookback windows. *Use case: Comparing first-touch and last-touch revenue for a campaign, widening the lookback window to match a long buying cycle, checking which channel drove yesterday's orders.*
- [toui URL Shortener](https://toui.io) **`C`** - Creates short links with UTM channel tags, groups them into campaigns, reports clicks by day, country, referrer and device per link or campaign, and edits link-in-bio page buttons. *Use case: Shortening a launch-post link tagged for a newsletter, comparing clicks by country across a spring campaign, adding a new product button to a link-in-bio page.*
- [TrendsAGI](https://trendsagi.com) **`C`** - Reports trending topics by category or brand with volume, velocity and trajectory, AI insights on themes, sentiment and audience, recommendations, crisis events and watchlists. *Use case: Checking whether a trend is still rising before committing ad budget, pulling marketing angles for a creative brief.*
- [Triggerbee](https://www.triggerbee.com) **`C`** - Reports onsite campaign conversions, signups, revenue and traffic, compares experiences across sites and periods, generates audiences from descriptions, and creates, edits, publishes or unpublishes popups, forms, surveys and personalization campaigns. *Use case: Comparing newsletter popup conversions across two market sites last month, creating an audience of returning visitors from a short description, updating a holiday campaign's targeting before launch.*
- [Two Minute Reports For Marketing Analytics](https://twominutereports.com) **`C`** - Analyze PPC, SEO, social, ecommerce & email marketing data from Facebook Ads (Meta), Google Ads, TikTok Ads, LinkedIn Ads, Google Analytics 4, Amazon Ads, Shopify and 25+ platforms in one place. *Use case: Comparing TikTok Ads and Facebook Ads performance side by side for the same campaign, checking Amazon Ads spend against Shopify sales, pulling an SEO summary alongside PPC data in one place.*
- [Uniqode](https://www.uniqode.com) **`C`** - Reports Uniqode QR code and Linkpage performance — scans, unique visitors, link clicks, and country, device, and time breakdowns — and builds custom dashboards. *Use case: Checking which in-store QR codes are scanned most, comparing two campaigns by tag, building a dashboard of weekly scan trends.*
- [Upbuild](https://www.uptimal.com) **`C`** - Provides read-only queries across ten marketing and analytics platforms, including GA4, Google Ads, Meta Ads, TikTok Ads, Adobe Analytics, and Google Tag Manager. *Use case: Comparing spend efficiency between Meta and TikTok last month, auditing which tags fire on a checkout page, pulling a GA4 channel report for a review.*
- [UTMKit](https://utmkit.co) **`C`** - Creates and updates UTM-tagged short links under workspace naming rules, organizes campaigns and tags, returns QR codes and click reports, finds broken links and publishes read-only client report links. *Use case: Building tagged links for a newsletter campaign, finding links whose destinations broke, sharing a click report with a client.*
- [Varify MCP](https://varify.io) **`C`** - Varify's MCP connector lets Claude create, manage, and pull results for website A/B testing experiments through natural language. *Use case: Ask Claude to set up a 50/50 split test on a pricing page and later pull the revenue results for the last seven days.*
- [Verso](https://www.askverso.ai) **`C`** - Reads qualitative research studies, briefs, interview guides, reports and participant answers, shows theme breakdowns, queries personas, designs and changes studies, sets study status and exports results. *Use case: Pulling participant quotes on packaging, splitting a theme by age group for a slide.*
- [Vibewatch](https://vibewatch.io) **`C`** - Reads web3 community sentiment from Discord, Telegram, X, Bluesky and other platforms, including vibe scores by platform, sentiment trends, daily highlights and lowlights, weekly reports, message search and market context. *Use case: Explaining a sentiment dip on Discord last week, summarizing the latest weekly community report, searching negative messages about a token launch.*
- [Visual Quiz Builder](https://www.visualquizbuilder.com) **`C`** - Reads Shopify product quiz structures, responses, metrics and customer insights, plus chat agent metrics and store details, so quiz performance can be analyzed in conversation. *Use case: Comparing a skin quiz's completion rate this month with last month, checking how a store's chat agent is performing, summarizing what customers answered most often.*
- [VTurb](https://www.vturb.com) **`C`** - Reads video sales letter analytics such as plays, conversions, retention curves, traffic and UTM breakdowns, heatmaps and benchmarks, creates, starts and manages A/B tests and traffic splits, updates player controls, CTAs, overlays, pixels and autoplay, and imports, uploads, duplicates and organizes videos and folders. *Use case: Ranking VSLs by conversions this month, checking which A/B test variant is most likely to win, adding a timed CTA button at the pitch moment.*
- [Weavely](https://www.weavely.io) **`C`** - Runs validated, read-only SQL against a marketing data warehouse in Google BigQuery that syncs ad, analytics and ecommerce data from platforms such as Google Ads, Meta Ads, Google Analytics 4, LinkedIn Ads, Microsoft Ads and Shopify, with schema lookups, client context documents and analyst playbooks for pacing, anomaly checks and cross-platform reviews. *Use case: Comparing Meta and Google Ads cost per conversion for one client last month, running a pacing check against budget, summarizing GA4 sessions by channel.*
- [Webz.io Blogs Segmentation](https://docs.webz.io/docs/webz/blogs-api-segmentation-mcp) **`C`** - Counts blog coverage of a topic in aggregation buckets by source site, author, organization and other supported fields, returning counts rather than posts or excerpts. *Use case: Ranking which blog sites covered an electric-vehicle launch most, identifying the authors writing most often about a competitor.*
- [Whatagraph](https://whatagraph.com) **`C`** - Query your cross-channel marketing data from 60+ sources — Google Ads, Meta, GA4, Shopify, and more — using natural language. *Use case: Comparing Google Ads spend against Meta spend for the same week, checking a GA4 conversion drop, pulling Shopify revenue by channel for a monthly report.*
- [Whooshly](https://whooshly.co) **`C`** - Creates short links, dynamic QR codes, UTM templates, digital business cards, link-in-bio pages, and lead-capture funnels, and retrieves link and QR analytics. *Use case: Generating a QR code for a print flyer, building UTM links for a newsletter, redirecting a live campaign link to a new page.*
- [Wingify](https://wingify.com) - Optimize digital experiences directly from Claude and supercharge your CRO workflow. *Use case: Reviewing which digital experience is converting best, adjusting the workflow before scaling it to more traffic.*
- [www.trendsmcp.ai](https://www.trendsmcp.ai) **`C`** - Returns live and historical trend data for keywords across search, video, social, shopping, developer package, entertainment and news sources, including growth, time series, top trends and top posts. *Use case: Comparing five-year search growth for two product names, checking what is trending on TikTok today.*
- [XICMO](https://xicmo.com) **`C`** - Reads website marketing data, including SEO, AI search visibility and traffic scores, SEO audit issues, ranked action items, recent agent runs, content drafts and credits, and answers strategy questions from workspace history. *Use case: Reviewing the top-ranked action items this week, checking which SEO audit issues remain open, reading the latest blog and LinkedIn drafts.*
- [Yogi](https://www.meetyogi.com) **`C`** - AI-powered consumer insights platform that analyzes product reviews, support tickets, and social feedback for CPG brands. *Use case: Ask which product attributes are driving negative sentiment in recent customer reviews.*
- [YPulse Insights](https://www.ypulse.com) **`C`** - Gen Z and Millennial consumer insights from YPulse, directly in Claude. *Use case: Checking how Gen Z shopping habits differ from Millennials before finalizing a campaign brief.*

## Sales Prospecting and Engagement

- [6sense](https://6sense.com) **`C`** - Get 6sense Insights. *Use case: Retrieving account insights.*
- [Actively](https://www.actively.ai) - AI agents that work every account across the revenue funnel. *Use case: Pipeline generation, deal execution, account prioritization, GTM sales intelligence.*
- [AdaptClose](https://adaptclose.com) **`C`** - Reads scored sales-call recordings and transcripts, searches transcripts, uploads and commits new recordings, manages scoring playbooks and checklists, and records door-knocking outcomes. *Use case: Pulling every call that scored under 60 last week, updating the scorecard to weight objection handling higher.*
- [Agentibus](https://agentibus.ai) **`C`** - Lists leads and conversations of an AI sales agent on Instagram and WhatsApp, approves or discards drafted replies, pauses or resumes the agent or takes over a single lead, and adds web pages or text to its knowledge. *Use case: Reviewing drafted replies to new Instagram leads before they go out, taking over a high-value lead by hand, adding an updated price list to the agent's knowledge.*
- [Airspeed](https://www.goairspeed.com) **`C`** - Finds customer calls and reads their summaries, transcripts, extracted insights, participants, and linked CRM identifiers, and can start agent conversations where enabled. *Use case: Preparing for a renewal meeting from past calls, pulling open questions a customer raised, reviewing commitments made on a recent call.*
- [Aligned](https://alignedup.com) **`C`** - Queries Aligned deal rooms for pipeline, engagement, and stakeholder activity, and creates rooms, adds sections, and updates mutual action plans after confirmation. *Use case: Pulling a pre-call brief on a stalled account, spinning up a room from a template for a new deal, adding next steps to a mutual action plan.*
- [Allegrow](https://www.allegrow.co) **`C`** - Improves outbound email deliverability with Allegrow: verifying contacts, warming inboxes, reporting spam rates and sender health, testing content, and searching connected mailboxes. *Use case: Cleaning a prospect list before a sequence, checking which rep's inbox is landing in spam, comparing two subject lines for primary-inbox placement.*
- [Alto](https://alto.video) **`C`** - Uploads finished videos and creates one tracked link per recipient, then reports who watched, how far, which chapters were reached, the most replayed parts, and where viewers dropped off. *Use case: Sending a demo video to three prospects with separate links, checking whether an investor watched an update, finding the part of a walkthrough people replayed most.*
- [Amdahl](https://amdahl.ai) **`C`** - Optimizes and evaluates outbound email and LinkedIn drafts against a company's own sales calls, notes and CRM data, researches buyers, and manages agents, evaluations and data connections. *Use case: Rewriting a cold email in a rep's voice, grading a LinkedIn message against past call themes, researching what a target account's buyers care about.*
- [anVendor](https://anvendor.com) **`C`** - Finds companies that use a given SaaS product or business service by location, industry and size, checks account lists for a vendor, and lists a company's services with estimated adoption and annual spend. *Use case: Finding mid-size logistics firms in Texas that use a competitor's payroll software, checking which accounts on a target list run a specific help desk tool, estimating a prospect's annual software spend.*
- [Apollo.io](https://www.apollo.io) - Find buyers and book meetings. *Use case: Sales prospecting, lead enrichment, outbound campaign management.*
- [Ask Nathan](https://asknathan.ai) **`C`** - Finds warm introduction paths into target accounts from a team's CRM, calendar and contact relationships with a 0 to 100 Path Confidence score and evidence, maps paths for pipeline deals and events, and after confirmation requests intros, logs interactions, merges duplicate people and imports people lists. *Use case: Finding who on the team can introduce a rep to a target account, checking the warmest path into each open deal, logging today's meetings.*
- [ASPR AI](https://www.aspr.ai) **`C`** - Prepares and recaps sales meetings with transcripts, MEDDPICC and sentiment extraction, suggested follow-up emails, pipeline exploration, and per-meeting coaching. *Use case: Prepping for tomorrow's discovery call, pulling follow-up email suggestions from yesterday's recap, reviewing coaching tips across last week's meetings.*
- [Attention](https://www.attention.com) - Sales conversation intelligence. *Use case: Analyzing client calls, CRM auto-fill, surfacing coaching insights and next steps from conversations.*
- [AutoRFP.ai](https://autorfp.ai) - Search your RFP content library, check live project status, and draft with approved answers in Claude. *Use case: Finding the approved answer to a specific compliance question, checking which RFPs are due this week, drafting a response ahead of a Friday deadline.*
- [Badger Maps](https://www.badgermapping.com) **`C`** - Is the #1 field sales platform. Listed as separate Standard and Advanced connector tiers in the catalog. *Use case: Managing field sales operations.*
- [Bidaya](https://bidaya.ai) **`C`** - Searches RFPs, tenders and firm knowledge for architecture, engineering and construction teams, including past proposals, resumes and project experience, summarizes pursuits and updates pursuit stages. *Use case: Finding open RFPs for a hospital project, searching past proposals for similar work, moving a pursuit to shortlisted.*
- [Bigmind](https://bigmind.ai) **`C`** - Turn meetings, CRM, pipeline, and company knowledge into answers and actions in Claude. *Use case: Checking what was decided in last Tuesday's meeting, pulling the current stage of a specific deal from the pipeline, answering a policy question from company documentation.*
- [BillionVerify](https://billionverify.com) **`C`** - Verifies single email addresses or batches of up to 50, returning validity, catch-all, disposable and role-account status with mail server checks, and reviews history, stats, async jobs and credits and manages webhooks. *Use case: Verifying a newsletter list before a send, checking whether a signup address is disposable, setting up a webhook for finished jobs.*
- [BirdSift](https://birdsift.com) **`C`** - Local business lead search over Google Places with website-quality flags, saved leads, CSV export, credit balance checks, and grounded cold outreach drafts. *Use case: Searching one city and category for businesses with no mobile-friendly site, exporting the saved leads, drafting an outreach email from the verified findings.*
- [BizFetch](https://bizfetch.net) **`C`** - Scores Japanese companies, government subsidies and public tenders against user-defined conditions using gBizINFO, National Tax Agency corporate number and invoice data, EDINET filings and Japan Patent Office records, creates, tests and compares conditions, edits target lists, marks candidates, watches daily changes, stores a company profile and products, and sends results to kintone, Google Sheets or webhooks. *Use case: Scoring manufacturers in Aichi against a target-customer profile, checking which open subsidies fit the company's own products, sending shortlisted candidates to a Google Sheet.*
- [Bizu.ai](https://bizu.ai) **`C`** - Searches and summarizes a company's WhatsApp sales conversations, including unanswered customers, open requests, AI sentiment and risk readings and service metrics by seller, with links to each conversation. *Use case: Finding customers left without a reply, comparing response metrics by seller, reading what a customer asked for.*
- [BoothIQ](https://getboothiq.com) **`C`** - Reads trade show and event lead data, listing events with lead counts and captures by rep, searching leads by name, company or notes, and returning contact details, notes, conversation summaries, next steps and recording transcripts, read-only. *Use case: Listing leads who asked about pricing at a conference, seeing which rep captured the most leads at a show, reviewing next steps for today's booth conversations.*
- [BOSRAI](https://bosr.ai) **`C`** - Reads your BOSRAI sales pipeline, results, buying signals and prospect replies, and builds outreach sequences. *Use case: Reviewing this week's prospect replies, checking which accounts show buying signals, drafting a new outreach sequence.*
- [BounceWatch Signal Tracker](https://bouncewatch.com) **`C`** - Lists dated, sourced company signals such as funding rounds, executive moves, expansion and launches for tracked accounts, reads company briefs, pipeline and outreach history, tracks or untracks companies, sets categories and updates action-board items. *Use case: Catching up on what changed across tracked accounts this week, briefing on one company before a sales call, marking a follow-up action done.*
- [Caretta](https://www.caretta.so) **`C`** - Realtime AI assistant that helps sales reps answer technical product questions during live sales calls. *Use case: Surface product documentation and past call context to prepare for an upcoming technical sales call.*
- [Cargo](https://getcargo.ai) **`C`** - Operates a Cargo workspace: runs connector actions across 130+ integrations, enriches people and companies, queries CRM and revenue models, and reads team playbooks. *Use case: Enriching a list of inbound signups before routing them, checking the ICP definition a team already wrote, running one action across a batch of accounts.*
- [Claap](https://www.claap.io) **`C`** - Searches Claap call recordings, summaries, and synced emails, and queries deals, companies, and contacts with their CRM fields and saved views. *Use case: Pulling next steps from yesterday's demo, finding every call where pricing came up as an objection, reviewing a MEDDIC view before a forecast meeting.*
- [Clay](https://www.clay.com) - Find prospects, research, and outreach. *Use case: Lead enrichment, personalized outreach, prospect research automation.*
- [Common Room](https://www.commonroom.io) - Community and go-to-market signal detection across social, product, and support channels. *Use case: Identifying high-intent prospects, tracking community engagement, surfacing buying signals.*
- [ConferenceDB](https://conferencedatabase.com) **`C`** - Searches conferences and trade shows by topic, city or month, and returns event dates and places, sponsor and exhibitor lists, published sponsorship packages, every conference a company attends and companies likely to sponsor a given event, read-only. *Use case: Listing the exhibitors at an upcoming trade show, finding which conferences a target account sponsors, building a sponsor prospect list for a new event.*
- [Connect The Dots](https://ctd.ai) **`C`** - Get a warm intro to anyone, right where you work. *Use case: Finding out who on a team already knows a specific investor, asking for an introduction to a potential hire through a mutual connection.*
- [Crayon](https://www.crayon.co) **`C`** - Reads Crayon competitive battlecards and competitor insights. *Use case: Preparing for a sales call against a competitor, handling a pricing objection, reviewing the latest competitor insights.*
- [Current CRM](https://currentcrm.io) **`C`** - Pulls the day's cold-calling queue with dial links, logs call outcomes, notes and follow-ups, adds new prospects, transcribes recorded calls, and answers reporting questions from call data. *Use case: Working through today's call list in order, logging a callback for next Tuesday, checking which area codes answer most often after 3pm.*
- [D&B Hoovers](https://www.dnb.com) - Searches Dun & Bradstreet company profiles, contacts, financials, corporate hierarchies, news and trigger events, technologies in use, buyer intent and website visitor data to research accounts and identify decision makers. *Use case: Mapping the corporate family of a target manufacturer before account planning, finding decision makers at a prospect showing intent for cloud security, checking recent trigger events before a renewal call.*
- [Deeplead](https://www.deeplead.io) **`C`** - Searches local businesses, companies and people, enriches companies by domain, finds decision makers, work emails and phone numbers, saves contacts, and runs inbox placement tests across Gmail, Outlook and other providers. *Use case: Building a list of dental clinics in Austin with owner emails, finding the head of procurement at a target manufacturer, checking whether a cold email lands in Gmail spam.*

- [trumpet](https://sendtrumpet.com) **`C`** - Builds and searches trumpet Pods and templates, and pulls engagement data, stakeholder activity, and deal-health signals from a trumpet workspace. *Use case: Building a Pod mid-call for a new prospect, checking which stakeholders opened the last one, reviewing deal momentum before a forecast meeting.*
- [Twain](https://www.twain.ai) **`C`** - Manages outreach workspaces, agents, campaigns, workflows and contacts, and generates personalized multi-channel outreach messages from real-time prospect research with dated, sourced signals. *Use case: Generating cold emails for a list of fintech leads, adding contacts to an outbound campaign, reviewing the signals behind a drafted message.*
- [Ubic](https://www.getubic.com) **`C`** - Reads sales call activity, qualification results, prepared calling sessions and their contacts, follow-up tasks and permitted call transcripts from a Ubic calling workspace, in read-only mode. *Use case: Reviewing qualification outcomes from yesterday's calling session, pulling the transcript of a promising discovery call, listing open follow-up tasks for the week.*
- [Unify](https://www.unifygtm.com) - Builds prospect lists, enriches contacts with verified emails and phone numbers, drafts outbound sequences, syncs to a CRM, and reports reply and conversion rates. *Use case: Finding past champions who recently changed jobs, pulling verified mobile numbers for a target list, reviewing rep activity over the last 30 days.*
- [Valuecase App](https://www.valuecase.com) - Search, create and update Valuecase spaces. *Use case: Creating a space for a new deal, searching for one from last month, updating it before a client call.*
- [Vibe Prospecting](https://www.vibeprospecting.ai) - Company and contact data. *Use case: B2B prospecting, company information, contact discovery.*
- [waitwho?](https://waitwho.ai) **`C`** - Searches a user's own LinkedIn connections by company, industry, role or seniority, returns person profiles for call prep, a daily outreach picture and post reach, and saves or updates LinkedIn post drafts. *Use case: Finding connections who could introduce a VP of Sales, prepping for a call with a contact tomorrow, saving a LinkedIn post draft for Friday.*
- [Zidi](https://www.zidi.ai) **`C`** - Finds Zidi screen recordings by what was said, reads transcripts and summaries, reports recording analytics and viewers who watched past a set percentage, checks campaign engagement, and creates share and tracked links and draft campaigns. *Use case: Finding the demo recording where pricing tiers came up, listing prospects who watched more than half of a follow-up video, creating a tracked link for one buyer.*
- [Zime](https://zime.ai) **`C`** - Answers questions across customer calls, CRM records, deals, and internal knowledge, returning evidence-backed responses linked to the source calls. *Use case: Reviewing which objections surfaced in a stalled deal, checking how reps positioned against a competitor last quarter, preparing points before a renewal call.*
- [Zoom Revenue Accelerator Connector](https://www.zoom.com/en/products/conversation-intelligence) **`C`** - Search conversations, analyze deal pipelines, review scorecards, and access team performance data — all through natural language. *Use case: Checking why a deal stalled in the pipeline, reviewing a rep's scorecard before a coaching session, searching a call for a pricing objection.*
- [ZoomInfo](https://www.zoominfo.com) - Enrich contacts and accounts. *Use case: B2B data enrichment, company intelligence, contact information, sales targeting.*

## Social Media and Influencer Marketing

- [1Social](https://1social.dev) **`C`** - Publish and schedule posts to Instagram, TikTok, LinkedIn, X, Facebook, Threads and Bluesky — and see what actually landed on each one. *Use case: Scheduling a product-launch post across every platform for the same morning, checking which platform drove the most engagement on last week's video.*
- [1stCollab](https://www.1stcollab.com) **`C`** - Influencer marketing operations covering creator negotiations, contracts, deliverable timelines, content review, usage rights, creator messaging, campaigns, briefs, and personas. *Use case: Working through pending creator offers with rate context, sending a submitted video back with notes, adjusting a deliverable deadline that slipped.*
- [AdaptlyPost - social media scheduler](https://www.adaptlypost.com) **`C`** - Creates, schedules, and publishes posts to nine social networks and reports views, likes, comments, followers, and engagement rate by date range. *Use case: Bulk-scheduling a week of posts with per-platform captions, publishing a saved draft to three networks, comparing engagement rate against the previous period.*
- [AdminHub for Telegram](https://adminhub.tools) **`C`** - Creates, schedules, edits and publishes Telegram channel posts through an AdminHub workspace bot, attaches images to unpublished posts, edits the text of published posts, deletes drafts and scheduled posts, retries failed deliveries, and reports channel subscriber statistics, with subscriber lists and profiles under a separate permission. *Use case: Scheduling tomorrow morning's announcement with an image, fixing a typo in a post that already went out, checking the channel's subscriber growth this month.*
- [Agentio](https://www.agentio.com) **`C`** - Reports creator advertising performance from Agentio campaigns, returning metrics with the definitions behind how each one is counted. *Use case: Checking cost per acquisition month over month, finding which creators drove the most engagement last quarter, confirming how a 30-day view is counted.*
- [Agorapulse](https://www.agorapulse.com) **`C`** - Reads Agorapulse organizations, workspaces, and connected social profiles, pulls content and audience analytics, and drafts posts and calendar notes without publishing. *Use case: Comparing engagement on last month's top posts, checking audience growth across two profiles, queueing a draft for a teammate to approve.*
- [Alvins Club Channel](https://studio.alvinsclub.ai) **`C`** - Connects social accounts, publishes approved existing videos to platforms such as LinkedIn and TikTok, creates, edits and deletes LinkedIn posts with uploaded media, creates and publishes Hashnode article drafts, checks post status and cancels queued posts. *Use case: Publishing an approved product demo video to LinkedIn, turning a blog post into a Hashnode draft and publishing it, checking whether a queued TikTok upload went live.*
- [api.blog2social.com](https://www.blog2social.com) **`C`** - Creates, customizes and schedules social media posts for multiple networks, manages connected network accounts, lists network properties and checks the remaining credit balance. *Use case: Scheduling a product launch post across three networks for next Tuesday morning, tailoring the copy for each network, confirming enough credits remain before a campaign week.*
- [api.breakreach.com](https://breakreach.com) **`C`** - Creates, schedules, and publishes posts across twelve social platforms including X, Instagram, TikTok, LinkedIn, Bluesky, and Reddit, with media upload and unified analytics. *Use case: Queuing a launch announcement into best-time slots on three networks, attaching a video to a scheduled post, comparing engagement across platforms a week later.*
- [api.influencekit.com](https://www.influencekit.com) **`C`** - Reports influencer campaign reach, impressions and engagement across Instagram, TikTok, YouTube, blogs and newsletters, creates campaigns and invites creators, searches creators by niche, platform and engagement rate, and diagnoses and refreshes posts that stopped collecting stats. *Use case: Summarizing top posts from a spring skincare campaign, finding TikTok creators with high engagement in a fitness niche, fixing a deliverable whose stats stopped refreshing.*
- [AuthoredUp](https://authoredup.com) **`C`** - Reads published LinkedIn posts from personal profiles and company pages with performance metrics, follower and connection growth and saved posts, creates and updates LinkedIn drafts with mentions for review in AuthoredUp, searches the help centre and manages roadmap suggestions. *Use case: Finding what the top-performing posts this quarter have in common, drafting a post that mentions a partner company page, tracking follower growth on a company page.*
- [Availnet Agency](https://www.availnet.ai) **`C`** - Searches a talent agency's creators by name or campaign brief and summarizes their channels, audience and performance, builds and shares rosters, checks availability, creates or edits ad inventory slots and recurring rules and records brand holds or bookings, reads and creates YouTube campaign reports, and adds public YouTube or Instagram creators to the agency. *Use case: Building a roster of fitness YouTubers for a brand brief and sharing it with the buyer, putting a hold on a creator's March sponsorship slot, summarizing a finished campaign report.*
- [Blabigo MCP Server for LinkedIn](https://blabigo.com) **`C`** - Reports LinkedIn post impressions, engagement and account totals with on-demand refreshes, lists drafts, scheduled and published posts, writes drafts, schedules, edits, reschedules, cancels and archives posts, and attaches images, videos and PDFs, with publishing only at the scheduled time. *Use case: Scheduling a three-post product announcement series for a company page, attaching a PDF to a draft, comparing impressions on last month's posts.*
- [BlackTwist](https://blacktwist.app) **`C`** - Schedules and publishes posts and threads on Threads and Bluesky, tracks engagement analytics, manages drafts, and sets up auto-reply follow-ups. *Use case: Queueing a week of posts across both networks, checking follower growth after a thread, clearing out drafts left from an old campaign.*
- [Brandwise](https://brandwise.ai) **`C`** - Searches, clusters and analyzes customer conversations across social comments, direct messages, email and live chat, breaks down comment sentiment, manages labels, assignments and internal notes, updates knowledge-base pages, and drafts or sends replies. *Use case: Clustering last week's social comments by complaint theme, assigning an unresolved email thread to a teammate, checking the sentiment split on comments about a product launch.*

- [Windsor.ai for Instagram](https://windsor.ai/connectors/instagram) **`C`** - Connect Instagram to Claude: query organic reach, engagement, follower growth, and post, Reels & Stories performance in plain language, then publish image posts. *Use case: Checking whether a Reel outperformed a Story this week, comparing follower growth month over month, publishing a new image post.*
- [Windsor.ai for LinkedIn Pages](https://windsor.ai/connectors/linkedin-pages) **`C`** - Connect LinkedIn Pages to Claude: query follower growth, impressions, engagement, post performance, and visitor demographics for your company pages in plain language. *Use case: Checking which recent post drove the most engagement, comparing visitor demographics against last quarter, reviewing follower growth before a hiring push.*
- [Windsor.ai for TikTok Organic](https://windsor.ai/connectors/tiktok-organic) **`C`** - Connect TikTok Organic to Claude: query video views, likes, comments, shares, follower growth, and profile views in plain language to see which content performs. *Use case: Checking which video got the most shares this week, comparing comments against likes on two posts, reviewing follower growth after a viral clip.*
- [Windsor.ai for YouTube](https://windsor.ai/connectors/youtube) **`C`** - Connect YouTube to Claude: query views, watch time, subscriber growth, engagement, and channel and video performance in plain language across any date range. *Use case: Comparing watch time between two videos from last month, checking subscriber growth after a new upload, pulling channel performance for a quarterly review.*
- [Winq](https://winq.ai) **`C`** - Reports post performance over 7, 30 or 90 days per platform, suggests posting times from past results, drafts posts in a team's brand voice with per-platform versions and media from Winq Files, searches and uploads files, edits drafts and scheduled posts, deletes drafts, and schedules drafts to publish. *Use case: Drafting a product launch post with a separate version per platform, finding good times to post on each platform, scheduling a draft for Friday morning.*
- [WoopSocial](https://woopsocial.com) **`C`** - Publishes, schedules and deletes posts across connected social accounts, validates posts, uploads and manages media, lists account posts and manages projects and webhooks. *Use case: Posting a launch announcement to LinkedIn and Bluesky at once, scheduling a TikTok video for Friday at 9 a.m., deleting a mistaken post.*
- [www.hiptrack.io](https://www.hiptrack.io) **`C`** - Saves post URLs into folders, searches a saved-post research library, reads channel strategies, drafts and updates content items, and summarizes recent post performance across Instagram, X, LinkedIn, Facebook and TikTok. *Use case: Bookmarking a competitor's Instagram carousel into an inspiration folder, drafting next week's LinkedIn posts against the channel strategy, checking which TikTok posts drew the most engagement last month.*
- [www.postel.app](https://postel.app) **`C`** - Drafts X posts, threads and LinkedIn posts from a voice profile and knowledge base, searches a library of high-performing X posts for examples, and saves, schedules or publishes posts through the official APIs. *Use case: Turning a client case study into a five-post X thread, finding high-performing posts on hiring for inspiration, scheduling a LinkedIn post for Monday morning.*
- [Xpert](https://xpert.so) **`C`** - Drafts X posts and threads in a user's voice, scores posts against ranking rules and past performance, suggests posts to reply to with drafted replies, finds leads and viral examples, and posts, schedules or cancels scheduled posts on a connected X account. *Use case: Drafting a launch thread and scheduling it for peak time, scoring a post before publishing, finding people on X describing the problem a product solves.*
- [Zamili](https://zamili.ai) **`C`** - Reads and replies to Instagram, Messenger, WhatsApp and Telegram conversations, pauses and resumes the AI agent per conversation, replies to and deletes comments, creates and manages comment-to-DM and keyword automations, publishes and schedules posts, and reads contacts, leads, orders and analytics. *Use case: Replying to customers waiting in the WhatsApp inbox, taking over a conversation from the AI agent, creating a comment-to-DM automation for a new post.*
- [Zaprep](https://www.011bq.com) **`C`** - Manages Instagram automation rules that reply to comments, direct messages, story replies, and new followers, including creating, enabling, and deleting them. *Use case: Spinning up a comment-to-DM funnel for a launch post, pausing a rule that fires too often, clearing out automations from an old campaign.*
- [Zernio](https://zernio.com) **`C`** - Creates, schedules and publishes posts across connected social channels and retrieves analytics. *Use case: Scheduling a post to several platforms at once, reviewing this week's top-performing posts, drafting content for a campaign.*
- [ZoomSphere](https://www.zoomsphere.com) **`C`** - Drafts multi-network posts with format validation, uploads media, moves posts through approval and scheduling, turns calendar Ideas into drafts, and reads per-post and per-channel analytics for Facebook, Instagram, LinkedIn, X, TikTok, YouTube and Threads. *Use case: Converting a client's backlog of Ideas into reel drafts, sending a carousel for approval before scheduling, comparing LinkedIn and X engagement for a monthly report.*
- [Zorevo](https://zorevo.io) **`C`** - Plans a week of posts around open calendar days and top-performing times, schedules, publishes, reschedules and repurposes posts across Instagram, TikTok, YouTube, Facebook, Pinterest, Threads, Bluesky and LinkedIn, reports analytics and recaps, tracks brand-deal contracts, replies to comments and DMs, and manages link-in-bio pages and notes. *Use case: Lining up next week's posts for one approval, pulling a brand deal report with every linked post's numbers, replying to unanswered comments from the weekend.*
- [ōrbita](https://orbitasolutions.org) **`C`** - Plans and drafts social media content for multiple companies using brand context, captions, a content calendar, and metrics, without publishing. *Use case: Drafting next month's captions for a client, reviewing a brand's content calendar, checking post metrics before planning.*
